  308: class ErrorBoundary extends React.Component {
  309:   constructor(props) {
  310:     super(props);
  311:     this.state = { hasError: false, err: null };
  312:   }
  313: 
  314:   static getDerivedStateFromError(error) {
  315:     return { hasError: true, err: error };
  316:   }
  317: 
  318:   componentDidCatch(error, info) {
  319:     console.error("App ErrorBoundary caught:", error, info);
  320:   }
  321: 
  322:   render() {
  323:     if (this.state.hasError) {
  324:       const msg =
  325:         this.state.err?.message || String(this.state.err || "Erro desconhecido");

   38: export function getApiBase() {
   39:   // 0) runtime override (sem rebuild)
   40:   if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
   41:     const runtime = stripTrailingSlashes(window.__API_BASE__);
   42:     if (runtime) return runtime;
   43:   }
   44: 
   45:   // 1) env (preferencial)
   46:   const fromEnv = readEnvApiBase();
   47:   if (fromEnv) return fromEnv;
   48: 
   49:   // 2) browser runtime (heurísticas)
   50:   if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
   51:     const { hostname, origin, protocol } = window.location || {};
   52:     const host = String(hostname || "").toLowerCase();
   53: 
   54:     // dev local
   55:     if (host === "localhost" || host === "127.0.0.1") {
   56:       return "http://127.0.0.1:3333";
   57:     }

   39:   // 0) runtime override (sem rebuild)
   40:   if (typeof window !== "undefined") {

   43:   }
   44:

  229: export function guessPalpiteFromGrupo(grupo, ctx = null) {
  230:   if (ctx && typeof ctx === "object" && ctx.palpite4 != null) {
  231:     const digits = onlyDigits(ctx.palpite4).slice(-4);
  232:     return digits ? digits.padStart(4, "0") : "----";
  233:   }
  234: 
  235:   const b = getBichoByGrupo(grupo);
  236:   if (!b) return "----";
  237: 
  238:   if (ctx && typeof ctx === "object") {
  239:     const dez = onlyDigits(ctx.dezena).slice(-2);
  240:     const uC = onlyDigits(ctx.unidadeCentena).slice(-1);

  275:   }
  276:   return 256;
  277: }

  279: /**
  280:  * ✅ Retorna a URL correta da imagem para o grupo e tamanho.
  281:  * /assets/animals/animais_<size>_png/<grupo2>_<slug>.png
  282:  */
  283: export function getImgFromGrupo(grupo, size = null) {
  284:   const b = getBichoByGrupo(grupo);
  285:   if (!b) return "";
  286: 
  287:   const base = getPublicBase();
  288:   const g2 = pad2(b.grupo);
  289:   const s = normalizeAllowedSize(size);
  290: 
  291:   // legado (se algum componente antigo ainda usar /img)
  292:   if (!PREFER_ASSETS_FOLDER) {
  293:     return `${base}/img/${b.slug}_${s}.png`;
  294:   }

  295: 
  296:   return `${base}/assets/animals/animais_${s}_png/${g2}_${b.slug}.png`;
  297: }
  298: 
  299: /**
  300:  * ✅ Fallback opcional (LEGADO): com sufixo do tamanho no nome.
  301:  * Só use se você tiver esse arquivo no disco.
  302:  */
  303: export function getImgFromGrupoFallback(grupo, size = null) {
  304:   const b = getBichoByGrupo(grupo);
  305:   if (!b) return "";
  306: 
  307:   const base = getPublicBase();
  308:   const g2 = pad2(b.grupo);
  309:   const s = normalizeAllowedSize(size);
  310: 
  311:   if (!PREFER_ASSETS_FOLDER) {
  312:     return `${base}/img/${b.slug}_${s}.png`;
  313:   }

   88: /**
   89:  * Retorna todos os horários conhecidos (flat + únicos + ordenados corretamente)
   90:  */
   91: export function getAllScheduleHours() {
   92:   const all = Object.values(SCHEDULES)
   93:     .flat()
   94:     .filter(isValidHourFormat);
   95: 
   96:   return sortHoursAsc(Array.from(new Set(all)));
   97: }

   99: /**
  100:  * Helper opcional:
  101:  * Verifica se horário pertence à loteria
  102:  */
  103: export function isValidLotteryHour(lottery, hour) {
  104:   const list = getScheduleByLottery(lottery);
  105:   return list.includes(String(hour || "").trim());
  106: }

   16:   return Number.isFinite(d.getTime()) ? d : null;
   17: }

   28: 
   29: export function isoPlusDays(iso, days) {
   30:   const d = safeISO(iso) || new Date();
   31:   const out = new Date(d.getTime() + Number(days || 0) * 86400000);
   32:   return out.toISOString();
   33: }
   34: 
   35: export function diffDaysCeil(fromIso, toIso) {
   36:   const a = safeISO(fromIso);
   37:   const b = safeISO(toIso);
   38:   if (!a || !b) return 0;
   39:   const ms = b.getTime() - a.getTime();
   40:   return Math.ceil(ms / 86400000);
   41: }
   42:

   44:    Telefone (BR)
   45: ========================= */

   32:   return out.toISOString();
   33: }

   36:   const a = safeISO(fromIso);
   37:   const b = safeISO(toIso);
   38:   if (!a || !b) return 0;
   39:   const ms = b.getTime() - a.getTime();

  437:       idx += 1;

  441:   }

  128: function extractHourFromText(text) {
  129:   const s = String(text ?? "").trim();
  130:   if (!s) return "";
  131:   const mh = s.match(/\b(\d{1,2})\s*(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)\b/i);
  132:   if (mh) return `${pad2(mh[1])}:00`;
  133:   const mIso = s.match(/\b(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::\d{2})?\b/);
  134:   if (mIso) return `${pad2(mIso[1])}:${pad2(mIso[2])}`;
  135:   const mBare = s.match(/(?:^|\D)(\d{1,2})(?:\D|$)/);
  136:   if (mBare) {
  137:     const n = Number(mBare[1]);
  138:     if (Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0 && n <= 23) return `${pad2(n)}:00`;
  139:   }

  417: /* ========= milhar palpite ========= */

  419: function digitFromKey(key) {
  420:   const s = String(key || "");
  421:   let h = 0;
  422:   for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i += 1) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0;
  423:   return String(h % 10);
  424: }

  429: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
  430:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];

  434: 
  435:   async function worker() {
  436:     while (idx < arr.length) {
  437:       const current = idx++;
  438:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
  439:     }
  440:   }
  441: 
  442:   await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, worker));
  443:   return results;
  444: }

   45: function toUTCDate(iso) {
   46:   if (!isISODate(iso)) return null;
   47:   const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-").map(Number);
   48:   if (!y || !m || !d) return null;
   49:   return new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
   50: }
   51: 
   52: function fromUTCDate(dt) {
   53:   if (!(dt instanceof Date) || !Number.isFinite(dt.getTime())) return null;
   54:   const y = dt.getUTCFullYear();
   55:   const m = pad2(dt.getUTCMonth() + 1);
   56:   const d = pad2(dt.getUTCDate());
   57:   return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
   58: }
   59: 
   60: function clamp(n, a, b) {
   61:   return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, n));
   62: }
   63: 

   65:   const dt = toUTCDate(iso);
   66:   const minDt = toUTCDate(minISO);
   67:   const maxDt = toUTCDate(maxISO);
   68: 
   69:   if (!minDt || !maxDt) return isISODate(iso) ? iso : null;
   70:   if (!dt) return maxISO;
   71: 
   72:   const clamped = new Date(clamp(dt.getTime(), minDt.getTime(), maxDt.getTime()));
   73:   return fromUTCDate(clamped) || maxISO;
   74: }
   75: 
   76: function daysBetweenUTC(aISO, bISO) {
   77:   const a = toUTCDate(aISO);
   78:   const b = toUTCDate(bISO);
   79:   if (!a || !b) return 0;
   80:   const ms = b.getTime() - a.getTime();
   81:   if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return 0;
   82:   return Math.floor(ms / 86400000);
   83: }
   84: 

   81:   if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return 0;
   82:   return Math.floor(ms / 86400000);
   83: }
   84: 
   85: function addDaysUTC(iso, days) {
   86:   const dt = toUTCDate(iso);
   87:   if (!dt) return iso;
   88:   dt.setUTCDate(dt.getUTCDate() + Number(days || 0));
   89:   return fromUTCDate(dt);
   90: }

    4: /**
    5:  * FiltersBar (Premium)
    6:  *
    7:  * ✅ Loteria:
    8:  * - filters.loteria: "PT_RIO" | "FEDERAL" | "LOOK" | "NACIONAL"
    9:  *   (default: "PT_RIO")
   10:  *
   11:  * ✅ Horário (domínio próprio por loteria):
   12:  * - PT_RIO: Todos + 09h/11h/14h/16h/18h/21h
   13:  * - FEDERAL: Todos + 19h/20h

   19:  *   horario -> "Todos"
   20:  *   animal  -> "Todos"
   21:  *   posicao -> "Todos"
   22:  *
   23:  * posicaoMode:
   24:  * - "full" (default): Todos + 1º..7º
   25:  * - "v1": trava em 1º (desabilita select e lista só 1º)
   26:  *
   27:  * posicoesFromOptionsMode:
   28:  * - "ignore" (default): IGNORA options.posicoes (posição é domínio fixo)
   29:  * - "respect": respeita options.posicoes se vier (com fallback seguro)
   30:  *
   31:  * Extras (freeze-safe):
   32:  * - disabledAll: trava todos selects (útil para modo DEMO)
   33:  * - onBlocked: callback quando usuário tenta interagir enquanto bloqueado
   34:  */

  475: function clampRangeToBounds(next, minDate, maxDate) {
  476:   const f = next?.from;

  484:   let to = t > maxDate ? maxDate : t;

  527: function getTodayYmdSafe() {
  528:   const ymd = normalizeToYMD_SP(new Date());
  529:   if (ymd && isISODate(ymd)) return ymd;
  530: 
  531:   const now = new Date();
  532:   const yyyy = now.getFullYear();
  533:   const mm = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  534:   const dd = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
  535:   return `${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}`;

  538: function yearRangeToDates(minY, maxY, floorISO, ceilISO) {
  539:   const y1 = Number(minY);
  540:   const y2 = Number(maxY);
  541:   if (!Number.isFinite(y1) || !Number.isFinite(y2)) return null;
  542: 
  543:   const a = Math.min(y1, y2);
  544:   const b = Math.max(y1, y2);
  545: 
  546:   let from = `${a}-01-01`;
  547:   let to = `${b}-12-31`;
  548: 
  549:   if (isISODate(floorISO) && from < floorISO) from = floorISO;
  550:   if (isISODate(ceilISO) && to > ceilISO) to = ceilISO;
  551: 
  552:   if (isISODate(from) && isISODate(to) && from > to) to = from;
  553:   return { from, to };
  554: }

  189: function normalizeUfToQueryKey(input) {
  190:   const s = safeStr(input).toUpperCase();
  191:   if (!s) return "";
  192:   if (s.includes("_") || s.length > 2) return s;
  193:   return UF_TO_LOTTERY_KEY[s] || s;

  209: function clampYmd(ymd, minDate, maxDate) {

  164: function isMoreRecent(a, b) {
  165:   const ay = String(a?.lastYmd || "");
  166:   const by = String(b?.lastYmd || "");
  167:   if (ay && by && ay !== by) return ay > by;
  168:

  170:   const bm = hourToMinutes(b?.lastCloseHour || "");
  171:   if (am != null && bm != null && am !== bm) return am > bm;
  172: 
  173:   if (ay && !by) return true;
  174:   if (!ay && by) return false;
  175: 
  176:   return false;

  494: async function runPool(items, worker, concurrency = 3) {
  495:   const queue = [...items];
  496:   const runners = Array.from({ length: Math.max(1, concurrency) }, async () => {
  497:     while (queue.length) {
  498:       const it = queue.shift();
  499:       // eslint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop
  500:       await worker(it);
  501:     }
  502:   });
  503:   await Promise.all(runners);
  504: }

   29:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   30:   if (!y || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
   31: 
   32:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map(Number);
   33:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
   34:   const mins = hourToInt(h);

   47:   const map = new Map();
   48: 
   49:   for (const d of Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []) {
   50:     const key = drawKey(d);
   51:     if (!key) continue;
   52:     if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, d);
   53:   }
   54: 
   55:   return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => drawTs(a) - drawTs(b));
   56: }

   20:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
   21:   if (n > 1) return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / 100));

  164: function rankTransitionMap(rowMap, basedOnGrupo, topN) {
  165:   if (!rowMap || !rowMap.size) return [];
  166: 
  167:   const total = Array.from(rowMap.values()).reduce(
  168:     (acc, n) => acc + safeInt(n, 0),
  169:     0
  170:   );
  171: 
  172:   if (total <= 0) return [];
  173: 
  174:   return Array.from(rowMap.entries())
  175:     .sort((a, b) => {
  176:       if (Number(b[1]) !== Number(a[1])) return Number(b[1]) - Number(a[1]);
  177:       return Number(a[0]) - Number(b[0]);
  178:     })

  166: 
  167:   const total = Array.from(rowMap.values()).reduce(
  168:     (acc, n) => acc + safeInt(n, 0),
  169:     0
  170:   );
  171: 
  172:   if (total <= 0) return [];
  173: 
  174:   return Array.from(rowMap.entries())
  175:     .sort((a, b) => {
  176:       if (Number(b[1]) !== Number(a[1])) return Number(b[1]) - Number(a[1]);
  177:       return Number(a[0]) - Number(b[0]);
  178:     })
  179:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, safeInt(topN, 3)))
  180:     .map(([grupo, freq], i) => {
  181:       const prob = Number(freq) / total;
  182: 
  183:       return {
  184:         rank: i + 1,
  185:         grupo: Number(grupo),

  182: 
  183:       return {
  184:         rank: i + 1,
  185:         grupo: Number(grupo),
  186:         freq: Number(freq),
  187:         prob,
  188:         probPct: Number((prob * 100).toFixed(2)),
  189:         basedOnGrupo: Number(basedOnGrupo),
  190:         totalTransitionsFromGrupo: total,
  191:       };
  192:     });
  193: }

 3263: function sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(m4) {
 3264:   const s = String(m4 || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
 3265:   if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return null;
 3266: 
 3267:   const dzRaw = Number(s.slice(-2));
 3268:   if (!Number.isFinite(dzRaw)) return null;
 3269: 
 3270:   const dz = dzRaw === 0 ? 100 : dzRaw;
 3271:   const grupo = Math.ceil(dz / 4);
 3272: 
 3273:   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
 3274: }

 3268:   if (!Number.isFinite(dzRaw)) return null;
 3269: 
 3270:   const dz = dzRaw === 0 ? 100 : dzRaw;
 3271:   const grupo = Math.ceil(dz / 4);
 3272:

  786: function probFromFreq(freq, samples, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {

  950: function clamp01(v) {

  952:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
  953:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));

  332: export function dezenasFromGrupo(grupo) {
  333:   const g = Number(grupo);

  713: function pickMinMaxFromMapped(mapped) {
  714:   let minYmd = null;
  715:   let maxYmd = null;
  716: 
  717:   for (const d of mapped) {
  718:     const y = d?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(d?.date);
  719:     if (!y || !isYMD(y)) continue;
  720:     if (!minYmd || y < minYmd) minYmd = y;
  721:     if (!maxYmd || y > maxYmd) maxYmd = y;
  722:   }
  723: 
  724:   return { minYmd, maxYmd };

  745:   }
  746: 
  747:   return { ok: false, maxYmd: null, error: null };
  748: }

 1570: function drawPassesLotteriesFilter(draw, lotteriesArr) {
 1571:   if (!lotteriesArr || !lotteriesArr.length) return true;
 1572:   const code = String(draw?.lottery_code ?? "").trim().toUpperCase();
 1573:   if (!code) return false;
 1574:   return lotteriesArr.includes(code);
 1575: }

 1599: function hourToNumSafe(hhmm) {
 1600:   const s = normalizeHourLike(hhmm);
 1601:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
 1602:   if (!m) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
 1603:   return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);

  253: function addDaysYMD(ymd, deltaDays) {
  254:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return ymd;
  255:   const [Y, M, D] = ymd.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
  256:   const dt = new Date(Y, M - 1, D);
  257:   dt.setDate(dt.getDate() + Number(deltaDays || 0));
  258:   return `${dt.getFullYear()}-${pad2(dt.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(dt.getDate())}`;
  259: }
  260: 
  261: function prevHourFromSchedule(schedule, targetHour) {
  262:   const sch = Array.isArray(schedule) && schedule.length ? schedule : [];
  263:   const t = toHourBucket(targetHour);
  264:   const idx = sch.findIndex((h) => toHourBucket(h) === t);
  265:   if (idx < 0) return null;
  266:   if (idx > 0) return sch[idx - 1];
  267:   return null;
  268: }
  269: 
  270: function prob(count, total) {
  271:   if (!Number.isFinite(total) || total <= 0) return 0;
  272:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(count || 0) / total));

   17:   const input = String(str || "");
   18: 
   19:   // 1) WebCrypto (preferido)
   20:   try {
   21:     const subtle = globalThis?.crypto?.subtle;
   22:     if (subtle) {
   23:       const enc = new TextEncoder().encode(input);
   24:       const buf = await subtle.digest("SHA-256", enc);
   25:       const arr = Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf));
   26:       return arr.map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
   27:     }
   28:   } catch {
   29:     // cai pro fallback abaixo
   30:   }
   31: 
   32:   // 2) Fallback via CryptoJS
   33:   // Requer: npm i crypto-js
   34:   const { default: CryptoJS } = await import("crypto-js");
   35:   return CryptoJS.SHA256(input).toString(CryptoJS.enc.Hex);
   36: }

   34:   const { default: CryptoJS } = await import("crypto-js");
   35:   return CryptoJS.SHA256(input).toString(CryptoJS.enc.Hex);

   38: /**
   39:  * userId determinístico e não sensível
   40:  * Ex: "cpf:ab12cd..."
   41:  */
   42: export async function userIdFromCpfDigits(cpfDigits11) {
   43:   const cpf = String(cpfDigits11 || "").trim();
   44:   if (cpf.length !== 11) return null;
   45: 
   46:   const h = await sha256Hex(cpf);
   47:   return `cpf:${h}`;
   48: }
